- **CSS规范**: - 字重不写精确数值；需要粗体时统一使用 `font-weight: bold`。 - `margin`、`padding`、`gap` 等间隔值必须使用项目的 `--gap`、`--gap-2` 至 `--gap-10` 变量，选择与设计尺寸对应的级别。 - `border-radius` 必须使用项目的 `--radius`、`--radius-2` 至 `--radius-10` 变量。 - CSS 变量只使用一次时直接写具体值，不额外创建变量；具有主题、复用或统一调整价值时才定义变量。 - 不自行添加 `letter-spacing`、`line-height`、`white-space`；确有截断、排版或设计稿要求时可以使用，并确保理由能从业务或视觉需求中判断。 - 已有 `line-height`、`white-space` 等样式若承担明确布局或交互作用，不得为了机械符合规范 ([lengyibai/tool](https://github.com/lengyibai/tool/blob/HEAD/agents/CSS%E8%A7%84%E8%8C%83.md))

- **ct-executor**: Cheap executor for mechanical edits already decided by the main session — a rename across listed files, applying the same change to N call sites, filling a table, updating a version string. Not for design decisions. Writes only the declared scope. Give it a briefing from `ct brief --role=executor --allow=&lt;files&gt;`. ([fcoluiz/context-tools](https://github.com/fcoluiz/context-tools/blob/HEAD/agents/ct-executor.md) · ★1)
- **ct-explorer**: Cheap read-only scout. Use for wide, low-judgment searches — where something is defined or used, which files a change touches, what a config says — when the main session only needs the answer, not the file dumps. Call it directly with the question; a briefing from `ct brief --role=explorer` adds handoff and map context. ([fcoluiz/context-tools](https://github.com/fcoluiz/context-tools/blob/HEAD/agents/ct-explorer.md) · ★1)

- **ct-map-keeper**: Cheap context-map maintainer.
